You build them with flexible systems that scale as you grow.

## AI that works for you, like you
Personalized, painless & productive: that’s how AI should be. In FUB, AI is built right into where you already work, and it gets you results by learning your business inside and out.
[Explore FUB AI](https://www.followupboss.com/features/ai)

[TRY FUB free](https://app.followupboss.com/signup/v2)[see a DEMO](https://www.followupboss.com/double-your-deals)
## Be ready for anything
The best businesses evolve fast to stay ahead of the competition. Traditional real estate CRMs and all-in-one solutions can't keep up. FUB is an open platform that gives you infinite room to build and grow—your way.   

[Why open matters](https://www.followupboss.com/open)

## The operating system for serious real estate businesses  

We help top performers in real estate break free from the constraints of traditional real estate CRMs and build thriving businesses. We know there's more than one way to achieve that goal, and that the best real estate businesses are constantly evolving and trying new things. That's why we believe that above all, those businesses need flexibility and choice, to be able to decide how _they_ want to evolve. Traditional, all-in-one real estate platforms limit choice by forcing customers to use the tools that they're selling and offering limited integration options. Follow Up Boss is different: We're a world-class CRM with an open API and vibrant ecosystem of integration partners, giving real estate professionals a flexibile toolbox for creating the business they always envisioned.

## Work with us  

We're a lean, nimble team obsessed with delivering for our customers, and we're always looking for more great people. Follow Up Boss is now part of Zillow Group. Join FUB at Zillow and enjoy these perks:  

  * 100% remote company - work from anywhere in the US
  * Competitive pay based on national averages
  * Generous health care coverage for employees & families
  * 401(k) with 4% match  

  * Generous vacation, sick leave, and paid parental leave policies  

  * Stipends for home office, fitness and more  

  * Annual company retreat  



[See open jobs](https://zillow.wd5.myworkdayjobs.com/Zillow_Group_External?q=%22Follow%20Up%20Boss%22)

You build them with flexible systems that scale as you grow.

Share story:
In 2018, Jason Mitchell closed more than $160 million in personal real estate production. Then he walked away from it entirely.
Why? To build something bigger.
Today, [Jason Mitchell Group (JMG)](https://thejasonmitchellgroup.com/) is America's [#1 real estate team by volume](https://www.realtrends.com/ranking/best-real-estate-agents-united-states/mega-teams-volume/), closing more than [$6 billion in sales and 12,000 transactions](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=t9YqP6BNLTQ) annually. But unlike traditional teams built on personal production or paid advertising, JMG's entire model is built on something different: B2B referral partnerships.
Jason saw the future early. "I made a conscious decision that I wanted to build a company," he explains. "I knew the company could create value and become valuable, whereas if it's only me in the field, it's limited."
## **The Challenge**
#### **Managing to scale across 180 markets while maintaining partner trust and agent accountability**
Building a team on referral partnerships is fundamentally different from traditional models. Jason's vision was clear: consumers were increasingly starting their journey with mortgage lenders, title companies, and other B2B partners. If JMG could become the trusted real estate partner for these companies nationwide, they could scale far beyond what any single producer—even a $160M one—could achieve.
But this model comes with unique challenges.
Speed and consistency matter to partners. When a mortgage lender or title company refers their client to you, they're putting their own reputation on the line. Slow response times or inconsistent service don't just cost you deals—they cost you the entire partnership.
Agent performance impacts market viability and centralized oversight is non-negotiable. With teams operating across 180 markets and 43 States, Jason needed visibility into every lead, every agent, every market's performance. 
## **The Solution**
#### **Centralized lead management and agent accountability through Follow Up Boss**
From day one of building JMG, Jason knew that traditional broker management wouldn't work.
"You can't build a company if 70% of your time is in the field," he says. His time needed to be spent on business development—securing and growing referral partnerships. But that meant building a machine behind the scenes that could execute flawlessly.
Follow Up Boss became the central nervous system for JMG's operations. 
> "With Follow Up Boss, agents can easily prioritize their leads, automate reminders, and keep track of all their clients’ needs. It’s like handing them a superhero cape."**‍**
#### **Centralized lead routing across 180 markets**
Every referral that comes into JMG—whether from a mortgage partner in Phoenix, a title company in Seattle, or a moving company in Tampa—**flows through Follow Up Boss**. FUB CRM [instantly routes each opportunity](https://www.followupboss.com/features/lead-routing) to the right agent in the right market.
This centralized visibility is critical. When a partner sends business to JMG, the corporate team can monitor response times, engagement, and conversion regardless of which market the lead lands in.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6452fc5f71b71358e5f0d512/67643cf33ac83f35acc4e70e_64a632091c888e32ab4d32d4_lead-prov-v2.png)
#### **Real-time performance monitoring**
For Jason, being clear on agent expectations is a non-negotiable. 
But how do you enforce that standard across 1,300+ agents in 180 markets in 43 states? Follow Up Boss's reporting gives JMG's leadership team [complete transparency into agent performance](https://www.followupboss.com/features/reporting).
"Most of our partnerships look at market area—how are you doing in San Diego, LA, Seattle?" Jason explains. If a market isn't performing, it doesn't just hurt that market's agents—it jeopardizes the entire partnership opportunity.
With Follow Up Boss, team leaders can quickly identify underperforming agents before affecting partner relationships. Response times, conversion rates, and activity levels are all tracked centrally, making it easy to [coach agents to perform their best](https://www.followupboss.com/how-it-works/coach).
> "We’re not just throwing a system at them; we're creating an environment where agents can truly thrive."
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6452fc5f71b71358e5f0d512/67eff0f439812bb700345295_64a5d61d4747faf08240f4c2_speed-tolead.png)
#### **AI-powered efficiency and oversight at scale**
With business across 43 states, JMG needed a way to evaluate agent performance faster. If Jason or his leadership team tried to personally review every conversation and interaction, they'd never leave their desk.
[**Smart summaries**](https://www.followupboss.com/features/ai) give JMG's corporate team the ability to monitor quality at scale. With AI-powered call summaries and lead history recaps, team leaders can quickly assess agent performance across markets without listening to every call or reading through pages of notes.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6452fc5f71b71358e5f0d512/69c6de7337702a9b7f309098_Screenshot%202026-03-27%20at%202.44.58%E2%80%AFPM.png)
[**Smart messages**](https://www.followupboss.com/features/ai)help JMG's agents respond faster without sacrificing quality. When a referral partner sends a client, the database-trained AI suggests personalized responses based on the lead's specific activity and history. This helps newer agents sound experienced, while helping all agents maintain the speed partners expect.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6452fc5f71b71358e5f0d512/69c6de86b356c151def4ba7c_Screenshot%202026-03-27%20at%202.45.23%E2%80%AFPM.png)
[**Lead prioritization**](https://www.followupboss.com/features/ai) surfaces high-intent Zillow buyers and qualified sellers automatically with AI-powered tags. Protecting those critical partner relationships by ensuring the hottest opportunities never slip through the cracks.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6452fc5f71b71358e5f0d512/69c6dee482e1689cb705505a_Screenshot%202026-03-27%20at%202.47.39%E2%80%AFPM.png)
[**Suggested tasks**](https://www.followupboss.com/features/ai) keep agents focused on the right follow-up activities. AI automatically suggests the next best action, helping agents maintain consistent follow-up without manual planning. When you're converting thousands of referrals monthly, automation ensures nothing slips through the cracks.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6452fc5f71b71358e5f0d512/69c6debd038cce90a24befe3_Screenshot%202026-03-27%20at%202.45.11%E2%80%AFPM.png)
For a business model built on trust, these AI capabilities aren't just nice to have—they're essential for maintaining the consistency and speed that keep partnerships strong across every market.
#### **Market-level insights for expansion decisions**
Follow Up Boss allows JMG to track conversion rates by market, by partnership source, and by agent. "We run these numbers annually," Jason notes. "Last year, our average agent made 700% more from us than what they paid into us."
This data isn't just for Jason—it's for the agents too. Transparency builds trust. "We're incredibly generous on our self-gen splits," Jason says. "I would rather pay you more on self-gen so you never leave than to start thinking you're taking too much from me."
> "Follow Up Boss isn’t just a CRM; it’s a lifeline. It’s the backbone of our operations that allows agents to concentrate on what truly matters—building connections."
#### **Diversified partnership tracking**
One of JMG's competitive advantages is diversification. "My largest partner that gives us business is still only 8.5% of my overall business," Jason notes proudly.
Managing dozens of referral partnerships across various markets requires sophisticated tracking. Follow Up Boss allows JMG to tag and track leads by source, monitor each partnership's performance, and quickly identify when a partnership needs attention or is underperforming.
"I don't want to be 80% of someone's business," Jason explains. "I'd rather be 60 or 50% of someone's business." The same philosophy applies to his partnerships—diversification protects the business and Follow Up Boss makes it manageable.
## **The Results**
Jason walked away from $160 million as a solo product to build something bigger—and it worked. In 2025, JMG closed $6 billion in annual volume with 12,000+ transactions. They operate in 180+ markets, 43 states, and have a diversified portfolio of referral partnerships that keep the business running strong.
But perhaps most importantly, they've built a system that agents want to stay in. "We don't really lose good agents," Jason says. "No one really wants to leave a situation where they're closing an extra 30, 40 deals a year."
> "We’re not just growing; we’re building a community where agents feel valued."
"You have to have your stuff together," Jason says about teams looking to expand. "You've got to have accountability in place, you've got to have reporting, you've got to have centralized everything."
The key to managing that scale? Centralization through Follow Up Boss.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6452fc5f71b71358e5f0d512/69c6d8367545d7a57425d370_Frame%201597879436.png)
With Follow Up Boss as the foundation, JMG maintains partner trust with visibility and holds agents accountable with reliable built-in data. "You can't build a company if most of your time is in the field. As an operator, you're the rainmaker. But you have to build a nice machine behind the scenes for partners to plug into."

Whissel Realty Group, a 120+agent top team in San Diego
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6452fc5f71b71358e5f0d512/6a2805734cd94e811831e876_whissel.svg)![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6452fc5f71b71358e5f0d512/67eff715ae6fcccfb672ca10_Case%20Study.png)
Website
[![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64662c9b89b1e1b97d4461e1_diagonal-arrow.svg)](https://www.followupboss.com/customer-results/whissel-realty)

Share story:
After making the leap from commercial to residential, Kyle Whissel has become an unstoppable force in real estate. As the leader of a consistently [top-performing team in San Diego, California](https://profiles.realtrends.com/team-profile?recordId=recdkeD2TDJuknSjh), and the US, he’s a big believer in building systems that will carry you to the top.
At Whissel Realty Group, it’s not unheard of to see 42 transactions scheduled to close—in one day. And they do it all on a foundation of strong systems and culture.
## The Challenge
### Aligning systems and culture
By the time Kyle started Whissel Realty Group, he’d finally found his sweet spot in residential real estate. The only problem? A chaotic business structure was wasting the team’s time. “We had meetings just to have meetings,” says Kyle. “There was no agenda. It was like, well, we should probably talk once a week.”
“We would hop on these meetings and it would be like, ‘Alright, who's got something?’ It was just such a waste of time.” 
No direction. No structure. And possibly too much vision. That all changed when Kyle found the EOS Model®. Kyle studied the real estate greats. To succeed, he knew he’d not only need to deliver listings and leads, but also leverage. 
With a clear business structure and a lot less wasted time, the only thing standing in his way was his ego. Kyle is the first to admit that building leverage for the team meant getting out of their way. 
“The second biggest change for me was being ok with not being able to get what I want,” he admits. “I kind of look at it like Veruca Salt from Willy Wonka, ‘I want it and I want it now.’ You go to all these events and [you come back with pages of notes for your team] you don’t realize they’re doing more harm than good.”
By developing a set system for prioritizing Kyle’s ideas, he prevents himself from doing damage to his team. It also helps him stay crystal clear about the things that are worthy of his attention.
> “If I know every little detail about every little thing, that means I’m not empowering my people to run the company.” 
Today, that team includes more than 120 agents and 16 full-time staff, and an international team of expert VAs. And all of them know exactly what’s expected of them. Better still, they have the systems they need to drop the juggling act and spend more time in dollar-productive activities.
## The Solution
### Providing leverage for agents, profitably
At Whissel Realty, the systems run the company and the people run the systems. And that’s where Chief Operating Officer Chris VanderValk works his magic. “As teams, brokerages, teamerages, whatever, we always prioritize leads—you need to give leads. But you also have leverage,” says Chris. The question for Chris was: _what if we switched it?_
Chris got to work figuring out new ways to provide agent leverage, while protecting profitability. With these activities as their non-negotiables—and with more than a little help from team Director of Operations, Shannon Youssefi—he created a brand new agent advisory board, transitioned the team to a no-cap model, and built out an Agent Concierge Experience (ACE) to consistently and profitably deliver more leverage.
And it all started when they decided to switch to an open lead management system.
> “I knew about Follow Up Boss and that was the pivot that quickly turned around database management for us. FUB gave us backend control over lead flow management.” 
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6452fc5f71b71358e5f0d512/676440372c015cac1d0bd36d_64a658a524111bab22d3615e_integration-graphic.png)
‍
### Building a strong sales foundation
The team started out using another company as their real estate CRM, but as the business grew and agents started losing focus—they knew they needed to make a change. As a third generation agent and former Listing & Transaction Coordination Manager, Shannon put both sides of her real estate brain to work and started systematizing.
> “If you want it to be an efficient process, it has to be a duplicatable process that you follow day in and day out—then you can deviate where you need to. We use Follow Up Boss as the core tool for the beginning stages, all the agents use that.”
With the ability to centralize leads from all sources—open houses, pay-per-click (PPC), Zillow Flex, and more—she built the backbone of the sales funnel that would eventually lead the team to its top-tier status. But with a transaction rate that reaches up to 42 closings per day, she knew she couldn’t stop there.
“For listing and transaction management, we use [Open To Close](https://www.followupboss.com/integrations/open-to-close), which integrates with Follow-Up Boss. This allows agents to see things in real-time which has been a game changer for us.”
“One of the things we have is a very clear form structure where from within Follow Up Boss, when an appointment gets set, the agent submits a very short form for that appointment. The form triggers different things—sending out a calendar invite to the client and putting it in our agents' calendar, it also updates our numbers for how many appointments we’ve had,” explains Shannon.
> “It updates the system automatically so that we have really solid data in terms of conversion metrics.”
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6452fc5f71b71358e5f0d512/67eff0f439812bb700345295_64a5d61d4747faf08240f4c2_speed-tolead.png)
She has automations in place for key milestones along the funnel, including after an appointment and when a listing gets signed. The result is solid data that helps the team understand how to make the margins work as they scale.
As a major bonus, this also makes life much easier for Rachel Choo, Director of Sales at Whissel Realty.
### **Using data to guide agent performance**
Rachel Choo is all about transparency. As the Director of Sales at a top-performing team, Whissel’s annual sales goal is her primary focus—which also means keeping a close eye on agent recruitment and retention.
> “What’s our sales goal and how do we get there? There's a lot of different ways that that plays out in terms of looking at numbers.” 
In Follow Up Boss, Rachel can drill down to the micro level for a deeper understanding of how the team is performing. As a former elementary school teacher, she’s big on using this data to provide feedback and [help agents grow](https://www.followupboss.com/how-it-works/coach). And as a former ISA, she has no illusions about what it takes to win. 
“A lot of times, income is a roller coaster for agents. As people who run teams, helping agents manage their own emotions and their clients’ emotions is important. Emotional regulation actually becomes a huge piece of why some agents are successful and some aren't,” says Rachel.
To reduce the overwhelm and make it easy for agents to focus, the team at Whissel Realty moved to a lead team policy. Instead of agents getting team leads from various sources, they're hyperfocused on either one source, or one way of getting team leads.
> “It really helps agents dial in. They know the scripting is the same for each one. We get to mastery a lot faster than ‘Hey, take this call from this one source.’”
Agents at Whissel Realty know what the lead experience is and are confident in their ability to convert. And they know they’ve got support when they need it.
Every week, Rachel tracks how many self-generated leads agents are adding to the pipeline, number of appointments met, number of pendings, and more. With a clear view of the right leading indicators, she can create high-impact training based on the real challenges agents are facing.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6452fc5f71b71358e5f0d512/67eff1207c67151a2693fae1_64a5e421cc0875eaf52372bd_leaderboard-v2.webp)
### Creating an ACE team
In the wild world of real estate, leadership at Whissel Realty knows you need to be ready for whatever comes next. Through the Agent Concierge Experience, they provide agents with the leverage they need to provide an unforgettable client experience, even in a fast-changing market. 
By outsourcing non client-facing tasks to a team of international VAs, tasks like writing offers, scheduling showings and updating the CRM are now completely off their plate. Not only does this allow agents to focus on appointments, prospecting, and negotiating contracts, it also gives them more time to sharpen their skills.
And that’s where Agent Success Manager Katy Carlson comes in. The former yoga studio owner and agent strongly believes in the power of building a cohesive community—especially now.
“In this climate, you have to set yourself apart as an agent because there are a lot of agents doing the bare minimum.”
Since day one, Kyle and the team have placed huge value on the role of the brand—both in their agent- and client-facing communication. And for over a decade, Media Manager Bryan Koci has been applying this philosophy to every piece of content the team creates.
Kyle brought Bryan in to help create the kind of ‘surround sound’ effect that captures audience mindshare, builds trust, and leads to better sales and recruitment outcomes. 
> “Structure, communication and trust. Without those, you can’t be a high-performing team.”
Like Bryan, Director of Marketing Max Bolanos has seen just how effective a multitouch approach can be. His main goal is to maximize the internal marketing team to become a full-fledged marketing team from start to finish.
“The same message has to carry through. With a multitouch strategy, the same start point has to get them to the same end point.”
While Bryan and Max make sure each touchpoint delivers the right message, Katy makes sure that message is embodied in their agents through regular roleplay and coaching.
## The Results
### Becoming a top team
Adrian Quijano is the [#1 agent at Whissel Realty by GCI](https://whisselrealty.com/team/adrian-quijano) and he’s got the track record to prove it. Adrian has doubled unit sales for 3 years straight from 15 to 30 to nearly 60 then 120. The secret to his success? Choosing the right team with the right systems.
> “It's kind of like a light bulb, right? When you plug into a team that has the systems, blueprint, resources and leads, you're going to light up.”
When recruiting agents, Adrian looks for a positive attitude, strong work ethic, and coachability. He knows from firsthand experience, that’s the way to win.
“When I first started, I looked up to the top four in the company, I call them the core four. They were consistently at the top and I wanted to be at the top. I figured the best way to do that is to be around them, ask them lots of questions, and learn.” 
Today, Adrian has built his team within the larger Whissel team, including 4 assistants, a listing agent, showing agent, and personal assistant.
> “80% of my activities are income producing—talking to clients, being in front of them, calling, following up. Those are the activities that I’m doing right now, only 20% is admin.”
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6452fc5f71b71358e5f0d512/67eff1a4859a549beea8712e_64a5baaafb20d0a19ef19a6c_overview.webp)
The way he sees it, this level of success wouldn’t be possible without the systems Kyle and the leadership team put in place. Instead of wasting time on admin, he’s out there paying it forward by recruiting future top agents.
“I wouldn’t be able to do 5 to 10 transactions a month if I didn’t scale my business and bring in the right people. I almost have a mini Whissel Realty Group within Whissel Realty Group, and Kyle allows me to do that.” 
Adrian’s not the only agent building their business inside the group. Karlee VanDyke closed $45 million in her first 2 years on the team. 
> “Now with FUB, you have all the systems and you don't have to, as an individual, go and purchase a CRM. You don't have to purchase office space or marketing collateral. It's all here.”
In addition to the incredible leverage the team provides, she attributes much of that success to the supportive environment at Whissel.
“Agent relationships and leveraging who other agents know is huge. If we're writing offers, we can speak to them and get a little more information about where the offer needs to be in order to serve our clients.”
Of course, it wasn’t always this easy. “I think when you first start or if you are lost, whether you’ve been in the industry for twenty years or one month and you need a reset, this team is the place to go,” says Karlee.
By joining a team that empowers agents to do what they do best, she’s skyrocketed her business while building the kind of relationships that will take her far into the future.
### At Whissel Realty Group, the sky’s the limit
When it comes to providing leverage and scaling profitability, Kyle and the team are just getting started. 
“The business I have today was not built overnight,” says Kyle. “It was built a quarter at a time.” It’s taken years, but Kyle and the fearless leaders at Whissel Realty Group have developed a scalable system that lifts everyone higher. And we can’t wait to see what’s next.

## The Follow Up Boss difference:  

![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e2f864115ade1b566dc9c_5efba33dd2608fec73643bcb_Frame%20267%20\(2\).webp)
### Agents love it
When someone tells you they're just starting their search and not planning to act for a few months, easily start a nurture campaign that keeps you top of mind while you focus on active buyers.  

![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/649cfd50ba75cbd927d1ed8f_Integrations%20graphic%20with%20bg.webp)
### Connects with everything
Technology should work for you, not hold you back. Follow Up Boss has an open API so you can connect it to the lead providers, marketing systems and other tools you use now, and any new ones you add in the future.  

![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/6598516d5691689fe0dcc292_team-photo-update-min.webp)
### Transparent pricing, no contracts
You have to earn your clients’ business every month. Why should we be any different? Follow Up Boss has clear pricing, no setup fees and no annual contract requirement. We help you close more deals, not lock you in.  

![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e043a5b3e9a2aba3ae08b_5f038d67d626305635426976_Frame%20274-p-1080.jpeg)
### Always working for you
Feel free to focus on real estate, not tech. Our support team is here for you 7 days a week via phone and email. Follow Up Boss engineers release updates and new features constantly so you’re always a step ahead.  

REAL CUSTOMER INTERVIEWS
In the past, I would say to new agents that you should be able to get a deal within 6 months. Now with Follow Up Boss, most are getting something under contract within 6 weeks!"
### Gary Ashton and Scott Hull
#1 Remax team in the USA/World
We're on pace to essentially double each year since we've been on Follow Up Boss. It makes it easy to run your team. It just tells you what you need to do. It's amazing.  

### Dean Devries and Mike Bacelo
Dean's List Group 5 star service
We can't accomplish anything without Follow Up Boss. We need it! We say it's our 'gold keeper.' It's the difference maker in everything we do.  

### Jenny Wemert and Emily Smith

# Calendar and client appointments
Manage all your client appointments and tasks from your computer or mobile phone
[Start free trial](https://app.followupboss.com/signup/v2)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663de8cff5e7e00a1ff69e_faq-hero-left-img.svg)![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663de8b9f0a97a1b997db9_faq-hero-right-img.svg)
2-WAY GOOGLE AND OFFICE 365 CALENDAR SYNC**  
**
## **Continue using the apps you love  
**
Keep everything in perfect sync with Google or Office 365 Calendar, get reminders how you always have, and enable your team to help you drive even more sales  

  * See agent's availabilities to set appointments
  * Updates automatically sync across devices
  * No need to switch back and forth


![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d444b114b31ffa0e41bec_Vector%20\(3\).svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a627166cb644441b8fc362_calendar-v3.webp)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d445e1c7ee0b5f9e40866_Rectangle%20988.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a6291ae51e8362075d79a5_appt-v2.webp)
CLIENT SERVICE
## **Keep client appointments organized  
**
Reduce no-shows and last minute cancellations and keep your entire appointment history on file  

  * Send invitation emails and reminder texts to clients
  * Events with client invited show on contact
  * Easily add team members to appointments  



TASKS
## **Stay on-task with your follow up  
**
Keep important activities for your leads and clients organized and prioritized whether you're at a computer or on the move  

  * Set reminders so you never miss a follow up
  * Sort tasks by type to prioritize
  * Automate tasks with Action Plans


![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d444b114b31ffa0e41bec_Vector%20\(3\).svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a62e142958e73937c07f93_cal-tasks-v3.webp)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d445e1c7ee0b5f9e40866_Rectangle%20988.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a62f5a896be48024e0b11d_report.webp)
REPORTING
## **See your success at getting in front of clients  
**
See how consistently you're able to get face time with your leads and clients to improve your chances at getting the sale  

  * See who is setting the most appointments
  * Know which lead source is performing best
  * Evaluate and your ISA's performance

# More efficient and more personal: That’s how you AI.
Work smarter with AI that knows you and your business.
[Get STarted](https://app.followupboss.com/signup/v2)[View Pricing](https://www.followupboss.com/pricing)
## Generic responses?   
New tools to learn? Not here.
Top agents are already using artificial intelligence tools to get an edge in their market. But you don’t want to look like everyone else, and “learn AI” is the last thing you need on your to-do list. Follow Up Boss gives you AI the way it should be: Personalized, painless and productive.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/684afc74d60621d1be8e1162_icon-drum.svg)
### AI that knows your database
FUB AI uses your most valuable asset–your database–to create a personalized experience for both you and your clients. No other tool can offer that. 
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/684afc74b49010084eaf1d8d_icon-sheet.svg)
### Right where you need it
Forget writing prompts or flipping back and forth between tools. In FUB, you use AI where you already work, with just a couple of clicks.
## “Right person, right time” has never been easier
### Smart summaries
Get up to speed in record time. FUB’s AI summarizes your calls and recaps the lead’s entire history at a glance.*  
  
*In beta
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/684b005e5c4a1685bbcac1cf_blue-triangle.svg)
### Suggested tasks
Never forget a follow-up. Important tasks are suggested automatically and activated with a single click.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/684b012f7c69f87c20aaeeb0_red-shape.svg)
### Smart messages
Send polished, personalized replies with suggested texts and emails based on the lead’s activity.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/684b0130b49010084eb1bf2a_yellow-shape.svg)
### Predictive lead prioritization
Know your best opportunities. High intent Zillow buyers and qualified sellers are surfaced automatically by AI-powered tags.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/684b0130e637f1184f08db11_blue-shape.svg)
## Powered by data that’s uniquely yours: conversations
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/684b05880bb623406bd02769_mobile-data-img.png)

## Frequently asked questions
What makes FUB AI different from AI in other real estate CRMs?
![plus icon](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646639bcf734448b1aecc455_Plus.svg)![minus icon](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663a11c0464764c2855c95_minus.svg)
The way AI is implemented in FUB compared to other CRMs depends on the specific CRM you’re comparing to, but in general:
‍
  * Because of its integrated calling and texting features, FUB AI has access to the contents of those communications. Not every CRM offers integrated calling and texting, and not every CRM that does is applying AI to that data.
  * Some other CRMs require you to use AI by prompting a chatbot with instructions. In FUB, AI is built into the places you already work, and you interact with it using a few simple clicks.


What makes FUB AI different from the big AI companies I keep hearing about?
![plus icon](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646639bcf734448b1aecc455_Plus.svg)![minus icon](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663a11c0464764c2855c95_minus.svg)
Third party AI tools can be great, but they don’t have access to your business’s data like leads, calls, texts and emails (unless you manually upload it), so the results you get are less personalized for your business. Third party models might know a lot about the real estate industry in general, but they don’t know much about _you_. Plus, third party tools often require you to work out of those tools directly, meaning that to use AI, agents have to toggle between FUB and a separate window.
‍
FUB puts personalized AI right where you’re already working, so you can supercharge your follow up with less effort.
How much does FUB AI cost?
![plus icon](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646639bcf734448b1aecc455_Plus.svg)![minus icon](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663a11c0464764c2855c95_minus.svg)
“FUB AI” isn’t a single feature that you buy. Our goal is to enhance the FUB features you’re already using with AI so they’re faster, more personalized and deliver even better results for your business. Currently, there’s no additional charge for AI features in FUB, but many of them rely on data produced by the [FUB Calling](https://www.followupboss.com/features/outbound-calling) feature, which is an added charge on certain plans.
Is my data safe? What do you do with it?
![plus icon](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646639bcf734448b1aecc455_Plus.svg)![minus icon](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663a11c0464764c2855c95_minus.svg)
The AI tools in Follow Up Boss are unique because they use data that’s already in your account, like calls, texts, emails, and lead activity, to help you follow up faster and more effectively. That’s what makes the AI feel so personalized: It understands your history and communication with leads, and surfaces suggestions that are relevant and context-driven. These tools use your data to personalize your experience within your account. We don’t share it with other customers, and it’s not used to train global AI models or any external systems. 
‍
All data is encrypted and stored securely. The [protocols](https://www.followupboss.com/security) we use to protect your data also apply here, just like the rest of your Follow Up Boss account. For full details, you can always refer to our [Privacy Policy](https://follow-up-boss-staging.webflow.io/legal-pages/privacy-policy) and [Terms of Service.](https://follow-up-boss-staging.webflow.io/legal-pages/terms-of-service)
‍

FUB PHONE
# Business phone for real estate
Never miss an inbound call, power through call lists and track every call automatically with AI.
[Start free trial](https://app.followupboss.com/signup/v2)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663de8cff5e7e00a1ff69e_faq-hero-left-img.svg)![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663de8b9f0a97a1b997db9_faq-hero-right-img.svg)
COMPANY NUMBER
## **Never miss an important call**
Get your hottest leads and active clients on the phone with the right agent, fast.  

  * Route calls to the assigned agent or a ring group
  * Use shared inboxes to collaborate on leads
  * Track marketing campaigns with unique numbers
  * Live transfer calls to agents or ring groups


[Learn more about COMPANY NUMBER ](https://www.followupboss.com/features/company-number)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d444b114b31ffa0e41bec_Vector%20\(3\).svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/688a928689a7429dd8d342ce_Agent%20Call%20Routing.png)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d445e1c7ee0b5f9e40866_Rectangle%20988.svg)
![graphical user interface, text, application
](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d377a1a7c5e6513bcbe91_60244debec54123a07f9c9fa_call-list-animation.gif)
OUTBOUND CALLING
## **Dial more leads, faster  
**
Increase agent productivity by placing outbound calls directly from the CRM.  

  * Dedicated FUB phone numbers for agents or groups of agents
  * Build call lists for your most important leads
  * Dial with one click from desktop and mobile
  * Automatic call tracking, recording and AI call summaries


[Learn more about CALLING](https://www.followupboss.com/features/outbound-calling)
![man smiling for the camera](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d3a88bedb5a135754d229_5f285830b5199d562366d2da_Eric%20Vertical%20Original%20\(1\)sadasd.webp)
_"Using calling lists, I can make 40 phone calls in 15 minutes. It's a thing of beauty."_
Ryan Fitzgerald
Realtor/Owner, [Raleigh Realty](https://www.raleighrealtyhomes.com/)
TEXTING
## **Calls & texts: better together  
**
Move faster and see the entire conversation in one place by texting directly from your CRM.  

  * Send rich text messages, including group texts and images
  * Switch seamlessly between desktop & mobile
  * View every text right in the lead history


[Learn more about Texting](https://www.followupboss.com/features/texting)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d4437c6662bba9c3aee38_Vector%20\(2\).svg)
![graphical user interface, text, application
](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d4493114b31ffa0e47507_6025c93759d2fce2146d8a8f_Calls%20and%20texts%20better%20together.webp)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64677ffef672d8ce2c1237fc_red-icon.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/66720dfe22316fbc79995247_ai-call-summary-webflow.png)
ENHANCED WITH AI
## **Get up to speed and craft the right message with AI  
**
FUB Phone uses artificial intelligence to record calls, summarize them for you, and suggest what to do next.  

  * Log and record every inbound and outbound call automatically to drive accountability and improve coaching
  * Report on call metrics for single agents or an entire team
  * Get AI summaries of individual calls or a lead's entire history
  * Deliver the right message faster with pre-written text message and email follow-ups based on all your previous communications with a contact


[SEE ALL FUB AI FEATURES](https://www.followupboss.com/features/ai)
INTEGRATIONS
## **Add more calling superpowers  
**
Want to add a multi-line dialer or text message marketing? Follow Up Boss makes it easy to connect your CRM with the industry’s best prospecting and marketing systems.  

[SEe Integrations](https://www.followupboss.com/features/calling)

# Start follow-up automatically when the timing is right
Automations help you act fast on new leads and uncover opportunity in the leads you already have. From the moment a lead enters Follow Up Boss to the moment an older lead shows renewed interest, Automations can trigger the next step automatically.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/69f390d14338bfb81b640542_automations-hero%201.avif)
## See Automations in action
## New leads get missed.   
Old leads get overlooked.
Most teams have two problems at the same time.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/69f3a5a6402ba372525403be_1.svg)
New leads
First, new leads come in and need immediate action. If nothing happens quickly, momentum disappears.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/69f3a5a687857e89d4494a6e_2.svg)
Older leads
Second, older leads sit in the database quietly until one of them suddenly shows signs of life again. The problem is no one has time to manually watch every contact, every activity change, and every possible hand raise.
So the result is predictable 🔮
  * New leads do not always get the right follow-up at the right time.
  * Existing leads with real potential stay buried in the database.
  * Teams spend money chasing more brand new leads while underworking the leads they already have.
  * Follow-up depends too much on memory, manual checking, and luck.
  * Opportunities get missed not because they are not there, but because no one saw the signal in time.


## A better way to catch the right follow-up moment
### Never miss the moment to act
Catch important events as they happen, whether it is a new lead entering Follow Up Boss or an older lead showing fresh activity.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/69f3d7b47bfffaf29f87d09f_never.avif)
### Get more value from the leads you already have
Stop treating your database like storage. Turn it into a source of active opportunity by surfacing the people who are ready for attention now.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/69f3d8e919ac7596f36d9cf9_get.avif)
### Keep follow-up moving without manual effort
Automate the repeatable next steps so your team can stay consistent without having to monitor everything by hand.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/69f3d9248ae64ba94b0b3fbe_keep.avif)
## How it works
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/697d11495aa8f3d86523e537_see-icon.svg)
Watch for the trigger
Choose the events or conditions that matter to your team, like a new lead entering Follow Up Boss or an existing lead becoming active again.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/69f3dd18a38f12a354b6d1db_pencil-icon.svg)
Define the next step
Set the response you want, like sending an email, creating a task, updating a field, notifying a teammate, or reassigning the lead after it is already in Follow Up Boss.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/69f3dd18af7894d0d6117e5c_arrow-icon.svg)
Let it run automatically
Once it is live, Automations keep watching your database and spring into action when those moments happen.
## Built to reduce manual monitoring
### Works across the lead lifecycle
Automations are useful both at intake and after the lead is already in your database.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/69f3dead8ae44d1a131593bc_diagram.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/6a0b80530235fd1c38f1c85b_works-mobile.avif)
### Sends emails automatically
Automations can trigger email sends as part of a workflow.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/69f3df24ca015b3746a662be_diagram-2.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/6a0b8053977e867471764c7e_buyer-mobile.avif)
### Supports reassignment downstream
Automations do not control distribution rules for brand new incoming leads, but they can reassign leads later once they are already in Follow Up Boss.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/69fe13ed6dbe73942888e23b_reassign.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/6a0b8053d091d400265c5ccc_reassign-mobile.avif)
### Built to reduce manual monitoring
Instead of constantly checking for activity, your team can rely on automations to watch for key signals.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/69fe1479f9ef6e60dc12534c_changed.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/6a0b8053964063f7239cee71_changed-mobile.avif)
## Where Automations fit into your workflow
![graphical illustration with a hand pointing](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/69f3e1c735c15a17037d9057_new-inquiry.svg)
### For teams focused on speed-to-lead
Use Automations to kick off follow-up the moment a new lead flows into Follow Up Boss.  

![graphical user interface, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/69f3e1c772f6332f9317caed_re-activated.svg)
### For teams sitting on a large database
Use Automations to watch for signs that older leads are active again and ready for re-engagement.  

![graphical user interface, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/69f3e1c7bb15a5454ffdcb7a_for-teams.svg)
### For teams trying to reduce manual process
Use Automations to create tasks, send emails, update records, and keep workflows moving automatically.
![graphical user interface, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/69f3e1c78517abc68cae79bb_lead.svg)
### For teams managing handoffs
Use Automations to support cleaner transitions, including reassigning leads after intake when the workflow calls for it.
## The opportunities were already there. Now your team can act on them.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/6a0603f28e6ac8959c4994b0_tiffany.png)
Before Automations, our team relied on memory and oversight. Now the system handles the next step automatically. Nothing slips through the cracks anymore.
Tiffany Gelzinis
The Gelzinis Group

TEAM COLLABORATION
# Work together for higher conversions and happier clients
[Start free trial](https://app.followupboss.com/signup/v2)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663de8cff5e7e00a1ff69e_faq-hero-left-img.svg)![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663de8b9f0a97a1b997db9_faq-hero-right-img.svg)
COMMUNICATE
## **Easily loop in relevant team members to support a client  
**
Give team members visibility into specific contacts so listing agents, buyer's agents and admins can deliver outstanding service  

  * @Mentions to communicate with teammates
  * Add collaborators to a contact
  * Work with your lender partner


![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d444b114b31ffa0e41bec_Vector%20\(3\).svg)
![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e773c591b20e03c24c233_5cd09707b5499f2655f76eb2_Frame%20\(6\).webp)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d445e1c7ee0b5f9e40866_Rectangle%20988.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a632f0740e455bdab9fde2_cal.webp)
CALENDAR
## **Avoid conflicts with shared calendar  
**
Get visibility into each other's availabilities to save back and forth and avoid double-bookings  

  * See busy times in Google Calendar
  * Set appointments and invite teammates
  * Assign tasks to other team members


CONVERSATIONS
## **Don't make clients answer the same question twice  
**
Get transparency into all conversations with a client across the entire team so everyone stays in the loop  

  * See full conversation history
  * Access important files and attachments
  * Tracked across all team members


![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d444b114b31ffa0e41bec_Vector%20\(3\).svg)
![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e77898c995ac42c2853e6_5cd090bfe7801df6e5c087eb_Frame%20\(2\).webp)

SMART ROUTING
## **Send calls to the right agent, automatically  
**
Don’t miss new leads or make your clients wait. Get them connected, fast.  

  * Route calls from current clients straight to the assigned agent
  * Send new leads to designated ring groups
  * Track missed calls and put them in a shared inbox for faster follow up

CALL TRANSFERS
## **Manage calls efficiently  
**
Clients and agents both win when call transfers are smooth and seamless.  

  * Live transfer calls
  * Forward to voicemail or another number
  * Set rules for during and after office hours  



MOBILE APP
## **Call from anywhere  
**
Agents don’t need to be glued to their desks to take their incoming calls. Follow Up Boss for iPhone and Android keeps them in the loop.  

  * Route or live transfer calls to mobile phones
  * See full caller ID for CRM contacts
  * Log calls automatically, capture notes easily

## Built-in tracking, reporting & call recording  

![graphical user interface, text, application
](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e043a30f4be9d1f0e338e_5d026a3453e6e67af6bbe53b_Frame15a%20\(2\).webp)
### Spend less time tracking
Details like date, time and duration get **logged automatically**. Easily capture call results and notes in real time.  

![graphical user interface, text, application
](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e0439a0b67a405712d8f6_5cd3236dcebd602e87321b11_Frame%20\(35\)-p-1080.webp)
### Know what’s working
See **reporting** on metrics like calls, talk time, conversations and appointments. Dig in by agent, team or lead source.  

![graphical user interface, text, application
](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e043a9387d16b005f75d2_5efb7b7343b299a4486a4c23_Group%20264%20\(1\).webp)
### Recognize hard work
Use the built-in **Leaderboard** to motivate your team with friendly competition. Track your monthly goals or host a call night.  

![people sitting at the table looking looking at the screen in front of them](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e043a5b3e9a2aba3ae08b_5f038d67d626305635426976_Frame%20274-p-1080.jpeg)
### Coach smarter
Activate **call recording** to record every call automatically. Review important conversations and use them for training and coaching.

EMAIL
# Drip, batch or 1-1: Every email in one place, tracked automatically
[Start free trial](https://app.followupboss.com/signup/v2)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663de8cff5e7e00a1ff69e_faq-hero-left-img.svg)![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663de8b9f0a97a1b997db9_faq-hero-right-img.svg)
Your agents are rushing to appointments. Drowning in paperwork. Revising offers. Managing a dozen clients on a minute-to-minute basis. And trying to connect with leads along the way.   
  
Email was supposed to make this all easier. But poor emailing in your current CRM has caused even your best agents to:  

  * **Waste minutes each hour** searching for email threads from months ago
  * **Look unprofessional** when a lead reminds them of emails they’ve exchanged
  * **Sweat through awkward excuses** when they totally forget to reply to a lead
  * **Get buried** under dozens of messages an hour (and fall behind on follow-up)  

  * **Struggle to manage** emails across browser tabs, their phone and a tablet  



![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a6f50826cfaff7727294d5_email-platform.webp)
ACTION PLANS
## Simplify and automate email with Follow Up Boss, featuring the game-changing system that organizes email by lead. So agents stay in touch with leads to close deals.  

![Wistia video thumbnail](https://embed-ssl.wistia.com/deliveries/a3e68d4d9a9610e09b8b13c12f8a4b09.webp?image_crop_resized=1280x720)
Use Gmail, GSuite or Office 365? Connect it to Follow Up Boss.  
Your team can email as they always have, and you’ll get the transparency of seeing the emails they send.  

![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e452b2ecd75ad62d5d2b8_5d027a18e5a5fe1905b0a25b_Frame22-p-1080.webp)
### See every email in one place
Your agents don’t need to scroll endlessly for an email or rely on their memory about what neighborhood or how many bedrooms a lead wants.  

![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e45290419da9beee82dce_5d027a36ea47b97220207204_Frame23.webp)
### Automate emails to new leads
Email new leads automatically, the second they hit your system. Use Action Plans to send emails on behalf of your agents, increasing speed to lead.  

![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e45293f3c91e7a7f7285e_5d027a3eea47b97235207214_Frame24.webp)
### Save time with batch emails
Want to send one email to a few dozen people on your list? Easily batch emails and send to a segment in a few clicks.  

![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e452a45570840ce903c73_5d027a438d1302009ec1f640_Frame25.webp)
### Eliminate guesswork and bad writing
Have a few emails in your back pocket that always get high open rates? Turn them into templates. So the entire team can send stronger emails.  

ENHANCED EMAIL FOR REAL ESTATE
## Use video, link previews, notifications and more. (Every single email extra your team wants is here.)

## **One central place for all your leads  
**
Pull in new leads from over 200 different sources, so you can manage your lead distribution from one convenient spot  

  * Consistent options for every lead source
  * One place to make changes
  * Easy to add and remove agents

SIMPLE AND POWERFUL
## **A hands-off handoff  
**
Set rules that automatically distribute leads to agents in the areas they serve and the price points they're suited to handle  

  * Distribute leads by ZIP code or town
  * Send leads from a listing to the listing agent
  * Give senior agents high-priced leads


ROUTING OPTIONS
## Prioritize speed or balance
Multiple lead distribution options to give your team a leg up on deals  

![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e6c916f386fb40ebdfa7a_62097282bc2e71a81c972653_ftc2%20\(1\).webp)
First to claim  

Instantly alert your team about new leads at once, and give priority to agents who can help first  

![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e6c91c70bcff042c52a91_6209723303559bef49ee6ab6_rr1%20\(1\).webp)
Round robin  

Give your team equal opportunities to new leads by distributing leads one by one, in a repeating cycle  
  

LEAD PONDS
## **Let your team fish  
for ROI from those expensive leads  
**
Give agents the power to discover more deals by assigning inactive leads to a lead pond  

  * Agents can prospect from pond leads freely
  * Create multiple ponds for different leads
  * Easily give agents access to specific ponds


![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d444b114b31ffa0e41bec_Vector%20\(3\).svg)

PIXEL
# Make your real estate website work harder
[Start free trial](https://app.followupboss.com/signup/v2)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663de8cff5e7e00a1ff69e_faq-hero-left-img.svg)![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663de8b9f0a97a1b997db9_faq-hero-right-img.svg)
DETAILED TRACKING
## Prioritize your best leads
Pixel gives you detailed info on each lead: What campaign they came from, which listings they viewed and when they’re looking at your site. Imagine if you could:  

  * Call your most active leads first
  * Find old leads that started searching again
  * See who's online now


With Pixel, you’ll feel confident you’re making the most of your time.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d444b114b31ffa0e41bec_Vector%20\(3\).svg)
![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e3f2b097cd68ffc9cab3d_5efa51011667fccfa77ad678_Frame%2033.webp)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d445e1c7ee0b5f9e40866_Rectangle%20988.svg)
![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e3f8249d70fa698425b07_5f036909ced1db7229426989_Homepage%20v3%2006-Jul-2020-140832.gif)
PERSONALIZATION
## Have better conversations
When you know the neighborhoods and price ranges a lead is interested in, you can walk into every conversation with a game plan. Plus, you can build drip emails, text messages and action plans tailored to the properties they viewed.  

![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e40176bdc94753f7e1537_5f17402d1760cd3e0bc4c1b0_Frame%20277%20\(3\).webp)
CALL TO ACTION
## Get more calls and texts
Use Pixel’s mobile-friendly call to action (CTA) to start generating more warm leads from your website traffic.   

  * Make it easy to reach your team
  * One-tap calling and texting
  * Integrated with your [Follow Up Boss number](https://www.followupboss.com/features/company-number)


You can even boost conversions by personalizing the CTA with the name, photo and phone number of the agent assigned to each lead.   

![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d4437c6662bba9c3aee38_Vector%20\(2\).svg)
![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e4095e07dba25a81c12d7_5efa512d680021ce808f6e22_5e3ad7b1b3f24d7931d7caef_pixel%20\(1\).webp)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d445e1c7ee0b5f9e40866_Rectangle%20988.svg)
![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e40eb64e7b1b85f2122ed_5efa51955deba6786c2a1f28_5e39db61888fab7e2e083825_Frame%2040%20\(1\).webp)
MARKETING REPORTING
## See which marketing campaigns really work
Using expensive marketing channels like Facebook ads, PPC or SEO? Pixel picks up lead source data automatically and gives you built-in marketing reporting so you can track ROI all the way through your pipeline.  
  
Facebook and Google just show you that you’re generating leads. Pixel connects leads to deals so you can spend your marketing budget confidently.   

EASY & FLEXIBLE
## Use any website (or websites) you want
Pixel installs in just a couple of minutes on any website platform that lets you add javascript.  

  * Supports major IDX providers
  * Track multiple websites
  * Automatically connect web forms


No other website tracking technology offers this much flexibility.   

![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d444b114b31ffa0e41bec_Vector%20\(3\).svg)
![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e413c4135a71158c53e9e_5f036dd6de9b6d9bcf2c0408_Frame.webp)
![man smiling for the camera](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e41539087c3943442820c_5fbc0fae32c4422062a64cef_tristan%20ahumada%20follow%20up%20boss%20quote%20\(1\)-p-1080.jpeg)
_"The thing we love about Follow Up Boss is the API integration with so many other systems. That's what makes Follow Up Boss so unique."_
Tristan Ahumada
Founder, [Lab Coat Agents](https://www.labcoatagents.com/)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d445e1c7ee0b5f9e40866_Rectangle%20988.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a634ffd65c2f2ec1ae06c0_pixel-capture%20\(1\).webp)
ULTIMATE CONTROL**  
**
## **Change your website. Keep your data  
**
Other platforms only offer website tracking if you use their website builder. Some hold your data hostage if you leave and you have to start over from scratch. With Pixel, you can use almost any IDX website you want, change website providers any time, and stay in complete control of your valuable data.  

## Why Follow Up Boss?
[ ![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/65a0591a50a851f4155d1830_fub-team-2023-web-500x230%20\(1\).jpeg) Unlike any other tech company Follow Up Boss is 100% real estate-focused so we can focus on a long-term vision for serving the real estate community.

Whether you need help getting up and running or have questions about your setup, we’ve got you covered.

REPORTING
# Track team and lead source performance for better ROI
[Start free trial](https://app.followupboss.com/signup/v2)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663de8cff5e7e00a1ff69e_faq-hero-left-img.svg)![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663de8b9f0a97a1b997db9_faq-hero-right-img.svg)
MEASURE RESULTS
## Evaluate performance faster
Track leading indicators like response rate to evaluate lead sources and agent performance faster than waiting for deals to close  

  * See if leads from each source are responsive
  * Track appointments set by agent or source
  * See if leads are slipping through the cracks


![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d444b114b31ffa0e41bec_Vector%20\(3\).svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/69e9013abcae6c3d9267165d_png.png)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d445e1c7ee0b5f9e40866_Rectangle%20988.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/69e90102ea4827d0ad7e545d_Frame%201597879453.png)
IMPROVE CONVERSIONS
## Increase your ROI from leads
Follow Up Boss makes it easier for agents to log their actions, so you'll now have a more complete picture for better decisions  

  * See if leads from each source are responsive
  * Track appointments set by agent or source
  * See if leads are slipping through the cracks


TOTAL CONTROL
## Close more listings
Use the ‘Properties’ report to see the effectiveness of your marketing and use that data to close more listings  

  * Show how many active buyers you have
  * Show how effective your marketing is
  * One click to generate a list of those contacts


![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d444b114b31ffa0e41bec_Vector%20\(3\).svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/69d92d4b256ba19b1f1c9fc0_Frame%201597879454.png)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d445e1c7ee0b5f9e40866_Rectangle%20988.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/69d92d671785568c8c148da6_Frame%201597879455.png)
TOTAL CONTROL
## Dig deeper
Drill down into your data with one click to see all the contacts associated with each metric so you can identify challenges and discover opportunities  

  * See list of contacts so you can filter further
  * Click on any contact to see more details
  * Coach agents and improve performance

Smart Lists
# Find and prioritize your best leads
[Start free trial](https://app.followupboss.com/signup/v2)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663de8cff5e7e00a1ff69e_faq-hero-left-img.svg)![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663de8b9f0a97a1b997db9_faq-hero-right-img.svg)
No one has hours a day to manually sift through 1000s of leads in a CRM to figure out who to call next. But how else can an agent sort the hottest leads from those in the Nurture Zone... so future commissions stop falling through the cracks or (worse) getting scooped up by the competition?   
  
The answer? Smart Lists. **The instant way to categorize high volumes of leads for follow up that never fails. Built into Follow Up Boss.**  

![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a5b7b66388b8af3b0210a1_smart-list.webp)
Improve performance
## Sort and prioritize lead follow ups.   
And turn every agent into a high performer.  

![Wistia video thumbnail](https://embed-ssl.wistia.com/deliveries/407fda80858c3c54f9aff39ee984bbc0.webp?image_crop_resized=1280x720)
Go from wasting hours on the right leads and wrong leads at the wrong time   
to spending just minutes on the right leads at the right time.
Routine
## Daily follow-ups start with Smart Lists.  

![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d445e1c7ee0b5f9e40866_Rectangle%20988.svg)
![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646f9c471c2c2f92ad97097c_5d02778d53e6e67b28bc3d6a_Frame17.webp)
## **Click on a smart list  
**
Save your most important smart lists for one-click access to your highest value prospects  

## **Click on the first lead in the list  
**
Start at the top. Review previous conversations, notes, or details about what they're looking at on your website.  

![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d444b114b31ffa0e41bec_Vector%20\(3\).svg)
![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/6474c6913375044a6d47936a_5d027794011a496cb5e16366_Frame18.webp)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d4437c6662bba9c3aee38_Vector%20\(2\).svg)
![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/6474c6d81eeb231dac06afe1_5d02779a011a49fe9ee16375_Frame19.webp)
## **Make notes  
**
Take notes from your call or add important details about your leads and clients.  

## **Move on to the next lead in the list  
**
Just hit the advance arrow to go to the next priority prospect on your list.  

![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64677ffef672d8ce2c1237fc_red-icon.svg)
![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/6474c738cde8661c3cba1256_5d0277ba011a49166be163f3_Frame21.webp)
![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/6474d13d1eeb231dac131a0d_5d027818ea47b9a650206b64_Frame5.webp)
TOTAL FLEXIBILITY
## **Turn anything you know about a lead into a Smart List. Yes, anything.  
**
Dynamic filters let you segment your leads without any effort. Leads visited your site? Put them in a Smart List. Leads saved 3+ properties as favorites? Put them in a Smart List. Leads searched for properties between $500,000 and a million? Put them in a Smart List. Then work through your lists. Done.  
  

### Add a “daily” tag to new leads.
When you’re trying to qualify a lead, add a daily follow-up tag. Later, remove that tag in a click.  

### Filter out old leads for nurturing.
Sort leads older than 3 months, and tag them to follow up with them less often.  

### Sort by last communication date.
Filter leads by last date, and set up a Smart List that identifies which leads to follow up with each day.  
  

SPEED & EFFICIENCY
## **Magically sort 100,000+ leads in a click. And find the buyers and sellers you need now.  
**
Customize Smart Lists in Follow Up Boss to clean up your contacts. Set up a “New Leads” Smart List, into which any lead created less than 7 days ago is filtered. Set up a “High Value” Smart List for leads searching over $1M. Or create a new “Relationships” Smart List by combining tags: leads tagged Past Clients plus leads who were Last emailed more than 60 days ago.  
  

  * Use pre-built tags
  * Add your own tags
  * Filter by multiple points
  * Segment 1000s at once
  * Create countless lists  



![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/6474d13daec6c339d1c6da70_5d0278548d130280bac1f0fc_Frame6.webp)
### **Filter by zip code + price tags.**
See which leads might be interested in that new listing you’re about to put on the market.  

### **Find nearly forgotten leads.**
Sort leads by “calls fewer than three” to create a Smart List of leads that need a call.  

### **Update your sellers on activity.**
Create a Smart List to remind yourself to email Sellers who haven’t been emailed in 5 days.  

STAY PRODUCTIVE
## For robust tagging you can’t find anywhere else,  
get Follow Up Boss, featuring Smart Lists.  

When you start your free 14-day trial of Follow Up Boss, you’ll instantly get access to  

  * Everything inside Follow Up Boss
  * Unlimited Smart Lists and master lists
  * Pre-built tags and segments
  * Custom tags and segments  

  * Exclusion-setting  

  * Segmentation and categorization across multiple data points / tags  

  * Unlimited lead sorting  

  * Ability to import databases of any size

# Follow up fast, no matter where you are
[Start free trial](https://app.followupboss.com/signup/v2)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663de8cff5e7e00a1ff69e_faq-hero-left-img.svg)![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663de8b9f0a97a1b997db9_faq-hero-right-img.svg)
## Smart iOS and Android Apps put your entire CRM in your pocket  

  * Make, receive and track calls and texts
  * Inboxes for different teams or locations
  * Access detailed lead data
  * Capture notes & collaborate with your team  

  * Track lead sources and marketing  



![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e09a66855f6c6000bc5df_5cd09af3f0a28d8f41f1529e_Frame%20\(8\).webp)
![man smiling for the camera](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e09fa6e9c70aecb695c9a_5f8873e2b6e9086ccb5c74bf_pg-quote-image%20\(1\).jpg)
_“All my leads finally come to one place, and all the texts and emails are in the contact record, no matter which device I'm using. It's fast and also super mobile-friendly. It has just the right amount of features - no clutter.”_
Preston Guyton
Founder, [EZHomeSearch.com](https://www.ezhomesearch.com/)
ENGAGE LEADS ON-THE-GO
## Go mobile.   
Because leads come in when your agents are out.  

![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e67e483d94c151e2e1534_5d0272a8011a49c9ebe1432b_Frame%20\(3\)-p-1080.webp)
**It’s like your agents never left the office.**  

Streamline tasks and move fast! Agents can hop in and out of the app whenever they’ve got a spare minute.  

![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e67e551fdbefa421e29cb_5d02720ce5a5fe0e52b070a5_Frame13-1.webp)
**Get notified the second a new lead comes in.**  

Push notifications alert agents when a new lead comes in, when they send a text and more.  

![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e681b10f5841de61f318a_5d02721be5a5fe4a4fb07365_Frame13%20\(3\)-p-1080.webp)
**Agent too busy for a new lead? @mention to re-assign the follow up.**  

If your agent’s running an event, they can @mention an admin to follow-up with a lead stat.  

![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e681b710e07588eec623a_5d0272258d13029bb2c1cf40_Frame14%20\(2\)-p-1080.webp)
**Have full conversations, including group texting - right inside the app.**  

Agents can make and track calls, texts and emails - and add notes - to any conversation. Without skipping a beat. Or longing for their laptop.  

![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e6857b8ff4a650cade5da_5d0273018d1302a861c1d713_Frame15%20\(3\)-p-1080.webp)
**View the listing your lead is interested in while you’re on the phone with them.**  

Live listing review makes it easy to check out the details of a listing a lead inquired about before you call them back. Or during a call.  

![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e684b285b642f3265a23e_5d02736d8d130217e2c1d7e2_Frame16%20\(4\)-p-1080.webp)
**Tap to call your best leads when they’re showing interest.**  

See which leads have opened and clicked your recent emails. Then tap to call them now.  

STAY PRODUCTIVE
## Arm each agent to follow up like a pro.   
From any mobile device.  

When you start your free 14-day trial of Follow Up Boss, you’ll instantly get access to  

  * Everything inside Follow Up Boss
  * iPhone and Android versions of the app
  * 2-way texting, including images and video
  * Group texting  

  * Email open- and click-tracking  

  * Task and appointment management  

  * Notifications  

  * Detailed caller ID  

  * Smart contact search (by name or number)  

  * Live listing views  

  * Recent inquiry details  

  * Mobile version of full follow up suite

# The easiest way to text clients
[Start free trial](https://app.followupboss.com/signup/v2)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663de8cff5e7e00a1ff69e_faq-hero-left-img.svg)![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663de8b9f0a97a1b997db9_faq-hero-right-img.svg)
INTEGRATED TEXTING
## The easiest way to send follow-up texts to your clients**  
**
Texting has been proven to get stronger engagement from leads and clients, so we built it right into your core follow up workflow.  

  * Quickly text from a client record or your inbox
  * Texts sync between your desktop and mobile phone for access anywhere
  * Auto-text your new leads
  * Send group texts, videos, photos & emoji 🎉


![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d444b114b31ffa0e41bec_Vector%20\(3\).svg)
![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e3244d1aafe1495c5ff5a_5fa1d939634be13d37b145fa_fub%20sms%20gif.gif)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d445e1c7ee0b5f9e40866_Rectangle%20988.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/69d697a34f5808ffb7a5b82b_Frame%201597879468.png)
EXCLUSIVE GROUP TEXTING
## Keep the right people in the loop**  
**
Follow Up Boss is the only real estate CRM to offer **integrated group texting**. It's just like the group texts you're used to, right inside your CRM.  

  * Keep both members of a couple up to date on a deal
  * Loop in your transaction coordinator or a lender
  * Hand a lead off to another agent


AUTORESPONDEr
## Text your new leads instantly**  
**
Get the fastest possible speed to lead by texting your new leads the second they hit Follow Up Boss.  

  * Initial text message sent automatically for new leads
  * Customize messages by lead source or type
  * Responses go directly to the assigned agent


![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d444b114b31ffa0e41bec_Vector%20\(3\).svg)
![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e333f29b7946e47d5ced4_5fd110fe422fa92f542779cf_Autoresponder%20text%20v2.gif)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d445e1c7ee0b5f9e40866_Rectangle%20988.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/69d92ae6b9377971d0027e78_Frame%201597879463.png)
TEMPLATES
## Improve texting speed and accuracy**  
**
Save your most common texts as templates so you can send them again and again.  

  * Supports 1-1 and group texts
  * Use our built-in templates or create your own
  * Avoid mistakes and keep your message professional & on-brand  



TIMELINE
## See the whole conversation**  
**
With texts sent right inside Follow Up Boss, the full conversation history shows up automatically, without any additional data entry  

  * See texts alongside calls and emails
  * Always know where you left off with a client
  * Full history to support and coach agents


![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d444b114b31ffa0e41bec_Vector%20\(3\).svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/69d92af76b344a34195bc847_Frame%201597879464.png)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d445e1c7ee0b5f9e40866_Rectangle%20988.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/69d92b0de1af4f25be571717_Frame%201597879467.png)
REPORTING
## **Track your results  
**
Texting is integrated into our powerful reporting tools so you can see exactly what's working  

  * See your response rate to texts
  * Understand which agents are most successful and who needs coaching  



TEXTING INTEGRATIONS
## Connect to advanced texting platforms
Your hottest leads need a human touch, right away. For top of funnel leads, tactics like drip text nurturing and ringless voicemail can work in tandem with your agents' follow up. **Follow Up Boss integrates** with best-in-class services that help you send high-performance campaigns while staying on top of deliverability and compliance.
