CAPTURE LEADS
## Route phone leads into one team workflow**  
**
When you send leads to Team Inbox, you can give specific agents or groups of agents access to them. Instead of just hoping someone picks up their phone, rest easy knowing that your whole team knows about every inbound call and text.  

![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d444b114b31ffa0e41bec_Vector%20\(3\).svg)
![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e0ac99c27033ea31b0207_5f84e269c0babd1c4cd6127d_Team%20Inbox%20Workflow.gif)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d445e1c7ee0b5f9e40866_Rectangle%20988.svg)
![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e56ee833cd1aedfac7eb7_5f84e2ccc900dfe082dd538f_Team%20Inbox%20Notes.gif)
CALL FASTER
## Collaborate to increase conversions**  
**
Respond immediately or assign a conversation to someone else on the team. Add notes or @ mention a team member. Schedule an email for later or activate an action plan. Team Inbox makes it easy for your team to work together and provide top-notch customer service to every lead.  

![man smiling for the camera](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e2cfe968dbd49ca717b4f_5e94bcfeabcb20986926d32f_Frame%2034.1-p-1080.jpeg)
_"In 90 days, we've already tripled the production of what the team did in an entire year. Using the system that's in front of them, they're able to dial it in, organize it, and do what they gotta do."_
Barry Jenkins
Team Owner/CEO, [Friends in Real Estate](https://buyingva.com/)
TRACK LEAD SOURCES
## Measure your marketing results**  
**
Assign dedicated numbers to your website, open house signs, digital ads and other marketing channels, then point them to Team Inbox so you can see how many calls each of your investments are generating.  

![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d444b114b31ffa0e41bec_Vector%20\(3\).svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/69cc1f06a38cc6bd341834fb_TRACK%20LEAD%20SOURCES.png)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d445e1c7ee0b5f9e40866_Rectangle%20988.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/69cc1e92881990d7b11411de_DRIVE%20ACCOUNTABILITY.png)
DRIVE ACCOUNTABILITY
## Stop leads from falling through the cracks**  
**
Team Inbox is designed to help your team get to Inbox Zero. It gives you a clear overview of all open communication so you can immediately see which leads have been followed up with and which ones still need to be delegated, deferred, done now or deleted.  

MOBILE APP
## In the office or out and about**  
**
Team Inbox works seamlessly between the Follow Up Boss desktop and mobile apps. An agent in the field gets a push notification about a new lead and can call back in real time or assign it to someone in the office for follow up.  

![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d444b114b31ffa0e41bec_Vector%20\(3\).svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/69cd459a2d98150cb538b78d_MOBILE%20APP.png)

# Engage leads with less effort
Make it easy for your team to work the right leads at the right time.  

[START free trial](https://app.followupboss.com/signup/v2)[see a DEMO](https://www.followupboss.com/double-your-deals)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a6b491e1c6af2535d2609b_Group-1171276791%202%20\(1\).webp)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/653bef0e597a2cb8424f2e23_Frame%201597879500.webp)
# Now your agents reach out on time and on brand  

No more dropped balls or leads slipping through the cracks. Maximize every opportunity and turn more leads into closed deals.  

Action Plans
## **Send an auto text & drip emails to leads**
Make sure every lead gets an immediate response from your team and ensure consistent, persistent follow-up.  

  * Personalize text and emails to increase conversion
  * Create a task checklist to systematize your process
  * Automatically prompt your team to reach out personally


[Learn more about Action Plans](https://www.followupboss.com/features/action-plans)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d444b114b31ffa0e41bec_Vector%20\(3\).svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a650606cb644441bbd32aa_action-plan-v2%201%20\(1\).png)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d445e1c7ee0b5f9e40866_Rectangle%20988.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a5b7b66388b8af3b0210a1_smart-list.webp)
SMART LISTS
## **Know who to follow up with and when  
**
Sort and prioritize your leads into Smart Lists, and turn every agent into a high performer. Remove the guesswork and stop wasting time on the wrong leads.  

  * Instantly see your most important leads and when they’re due for a touch
  * Dynamic filters let you segment leads without effort
  * Eliminate missed opportunities


[Learn more about smart lists](https://www.followupboss.com/features/smart-list)
![man smiling for the camera](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a5f920ef5125a1da100df7_daniel-dixon.webp)
_"We have the system, we have the tools, we have the processes. We have all the things that work and that are duplicatable in multiple markets.”_
Daniel Dixon
Team Lead, [Dixon Group](https://www.findcohomes.com/)
# Seamless communication
Engage your leads across communication channels, including in-app calling, texting, and emailing. All communication logs on a single timeline for each lead, allowing for more organized interactions and conversation history.
DIALER & CALLING
## **Make more dials & get calls to the right agent**
Power through call lists and track every call automatically with a complete real estate phone system built right into FUB.  

  * Increase agent productivity and efficiency with one-click dialing
  * Get inbound calls on the phone with the right agent, fast
  * Make and receive calls from your computer or phone
  * Get AI-powered call summaries & transcriptions automatically  



[Learn more about calling](https://www.followupboss.com/features/calling)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d4437c6662bba9c3aee38_Vector%20\(2\).svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a5baaafb20d0a19ef19a6c_overview.webp)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64677ffef672d8ce2c1237fc_red-icon.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a5db6c95b01b9ae26154aa_text-v2.webp)
TEXTING
## **Send and receive texts from your central hub  
**
Attract higher engagement and convert more leads into loyal clients with native two-way texting.  

  * Auto-text your new leads
  * Send video texts to build rapport faster
  * Keep related clients up to date with group texting
  * Use text templates to improve speed and consistency


[Learn more about texting](https://www.followupboss.com/features/texting)
EMAILING
## **Capture more attention with better emails  
**
Automate, track and simplify emails for your agents, and get transparency into what’s driving conversion.  

  * Send automated drip emails
  * See when people open and click your emails
  * Reach more people with batch emailing
  * Use email templates to increase open and reply rates


[Learn more about emailing](https://www.followupboss.com/features/email)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64677ffe543d669d8bc8a8e6_yellow-icon.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a5dcd27bf1426b2052fd29_batchemail-v2.webp)
![man smiling for the camera](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e2ecd2d2671e4a894ee6a_626870cd76e22ab6535d9de3_Albert%20Testimonial-p-1080.webp)
_"Automations have leveled up our follow up by automatically sending highly relevant messages at the perfect time. It’s easy to use, yet very powerful with all the triggers and conditions. Huge win."_
Albert Vasquez
Team Leader, [AV Home Experts](https://www.avhomeexperts.com/)
# Instant, consistent follow up
Every prospect gets the right combination of personalized and automated follow up. No more hoping agents follow the process – the process is built right in.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a5ddd5315cc16c3f716f3f_Automation%20\(1\)%20\(1\).png)![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64677ffeda2f60336b8d0a61_blue-icon%20\(2\).svg)
Automations
## **Find more deals in your database  
**
Automatically watch over your existing leads and spring into action when the time is right.  

  * Start an Action Plan when old leads make a new inquiry or save a listing on your website.
  * Send a happy birthday email on your client’s special day
  * Request a review when a client’s deal closes


[Learn more about automations](https://www.followupboss.com/features/automations)
TEAM INBOXES
## **Never miss a call or text to your business  
**
Capture and track incoming calls, texts and emails to your business, so you can prioritize first-class service.  

  * Route phone leads into one team workflow
  * Collaborate and respond immediately to increase conversions
  * Assign dedicated numbers to your marketing channels and measure your results

# Agent productivity unleashed
Follow Up Boss gives agents a fast, intuitive CRM that lets agents fly through the workday.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663de8cff5e7e00a1ff69e_faq-hero-left-img.svg)![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663de8b9f0a97a1b997db9_faq-hero-right-img.svg)
LEAD DISTRIBUTION**  
**
## Route leads to the right people
Follow Up Boss gives you control over lead distribution, so agents get the leads they’re supposed to, instantly.  

  * Sync leads from any lead source
  * Round robin and first to claim for the whole team or groups of agents
  * Customize by lead source, lead type, price range ZIP code and more


[learn more about lead distribution](https://www.followupboss.com/features/lead-routing)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d444b114b31ffa0e41bec_Vector%20\(3\).svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a633f915899abfa31c9023_Lead-distribution.webp)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d445e1c7ee0b5f9e40866_Rectangle%20988.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a63023b22dc2d1b5bd93ab_smartlist-and-activity.webp)
SMART LISTS**  
**
## Prioritize your best opportunities
Build Smart Lists that automatically find leads with new activity, overdue follow up, an important anniversary or other signals that it’s time to get in touch.  

  * Dynamic lists automatically update
  * Dial or email the entire list with one click
  * Start every day with your leads already prioritized for you  



[Learn more about smart lists](https://www.followupboss.com/features/smart-list)
![man smiling for the camera](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646fcf2582076b5e43656798_d.jpeg)
_“I’ve tried other CRMs but none of them could give me the simplicity and the power and the ease of making it all just work. I just want to sell real estate and Follow Up Boss has done that for me.”_
Paul Reddam
Owner, [Homesville Real Estate](https://www.homesville.com/)  

[READ more customer results](https://www.followupboss.com/customer-stories)
SEAMLESS COMMUNICATIONS
## Follow up faster and stay organized  

![graphical user interface, text, application
](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e02f7c6789932add46dc6_60245b7abe9e0d15cced8f14_Mobile%20calling%20screenshot.webp)
### Built-in dialer
Call any contact with a singe click, or queue up a call list and power through it in no time.   

[Calling & dialer →](https://www.followupboss.com/features/calling)
![graphical user interface, text, application
](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d4493114b31ffa0e47507_6025c93759d2fce2146d8a8f_Calls%20and%20texts%20better%20together.webp)
### Integrated texting
Send text messages, including group and video texts, to contacts directly from your CRM  

[Texting →](https://www.followupboss.com/features/texting)
![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/6476600663226c6001140c3c_63c9cdd3e77a4c1b71a50cca_email-sync%20\(1\).webp)
### Email with ease
Sync your Google or Microsoft email and send drip, batch and 1-1 emails with ease.  

[Email →](https://www.followupboss.com/features/email)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a5ca5964c24bd1865b929d_timeline1.webp)
### Every communication in one place
Every call, text and email your team has had with a contact gets logged on one timeline, so their complete history is always right in front of you.  

MOBILE APPS
## Make it mobile
Follow Up Boss for iOS and Android keep you on top of everything no matter where your day takes you.  

  * Push notifications for new leads
  * Your whole database in your pocket
  * Call, text and email your contacts, ping your teammates


[Learn more about Mobile Apps](https://www.followupboss.com/features/mobile-apps)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64677ffe543d669d8bc8a8e6_yellow-icon.svg)
![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e09a66855f6c6000bc5df_5cd09af3f0a28d8f41f1529e_Frame%20\(8\).webp)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d445e1c7ee0b5f9e40866_Rectangle%20988.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a63fc824111bab22b4374c_tam-collaboration-full.webp)
TEAM COLLAboRATION**  
**
## Collaborate to close
Give other team members visibility into select leads so they can help you close a deal and deliver an amazing client experience.   

  * @ mentions to communicate with teammates
  * Add collaborators to leads
  * Bring a lender into a deal  



[more about team collaboration](https://www.followupboss.com/features/collaboration-tools)
reporting
## Stay on top of your progress
Everything in Follow Up Boss is logged automatically, so it's easy to see how you're tracking toward your goals and how you compare to your team.  

  * Detailed reporting on all your metrics
  * Track deals and commissions
  * Leaderboard to share stats and create friendly competition


[Learn more about REPORTING](https://www.followupboss.com/features/reporting)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64677ffe543d669d8bc8a8e6_yellow-icon.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a6401ad2e8cb8b1b7522d2_Agent%20Report.webp)

# Organize your business around one central hub
A home for everything, so you can turn chaos to calm.  

[START free trial](https://app.followupboss.com/signup/v2)[see a DEMO](https://www.followupboss.com/double-your-deals)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a6afc87874977dd3aa4caf_organize-hero-img.webp)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a6b066b22dc2d1b55ab549_Pattern%20bottom%20White.webp)
## Never lose another lead
Pick your favorite lead sources and FUB will keep them all organized, give your team a central place to follow up, and track everything that happens.
LEAD SOURCES
## **Connect your leads from every source**
Pull in leads automatically from 250+ lead sources, so you can manage your own personalized client database within a single OS.  

  * Capture and follow up with leads from one place
  * Keep full control of your client data on your terms
  * Choose the lead providers that work for you


[see lead sources](https://www.followupboss.com/integrations?tag=Lead+Providers)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d444b114b31ffa0e41bec_Vector%20\(3\).svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a632091c888e32ab4d32d4_lead-prov-v2.png)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d445e1c7ee0b5f9e40866_Rectangle%20988.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a5c61c0f619c6fac0223cf_distro%20\(1\).webp)
LEAD DISTRIBUTION
## **Get every lead to the right person  
**
Manage and automate lead assignment rules to keep your team organized and productive even if you’re away.  

  * Improve speed to lead and client satisfaction
  * Assign leads to your team via round robin or first to claim
  * Distribute leads by area, price or MLS number
  * Organize lead ponds to let agents discover more deals


[Learn more about lead distribution](https://www.followupboss.com/features/lead-routing)
pixel
## **Sync your website leads & see their activity  
**
Pixel gives you detailed info on each lead: What campaign they came from, which listings they viewed and when they’re looking at your site.  

  * See when clients are on your website right now
  * Use any website (or websites) you want
  * Keep your data, even if you change websites
  * Track what listings clients view and save


[Learn more about pixel](https://www.followupboss.com/features/pixel)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d4437c6662bba9c3aee38_Vector%20\(2\).svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a5c886f724bb41dfbd7dd9_pixel-graphic%20\(1\).webp)
![man smiling for the camera](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/649dcea91ccfc8ef284a95ec_emilysmith.webp)
_"We call Follow Up Boss our gold mine. It’s our hub where all of the good things live.”_
Emily Smith
COO, [Wemert Group Realty](https://wemertgrouprealty.com/)
# Get everyone working in one system
Once all your leads and tech tools are connected, FUB becomes your team’s command center. You and your agents can run your entire day out of one platform.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64677ffef672d8ce2c1237fc_red-icon.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a5ca5964c24bd1865b929d_timeline1.webp)
COMMUNICATION HUB
## Stay on top of every conversation
Keep a cohesive record of every call, text and email between your team and clients, so you can have better conversations.  

  * View calls, texts and emails on a single timeline
  * Check one inbox to see your most important replies
  * Get AI-powered call summaries & transcriptions automatically


[Learn more about calling](https://www.followupboss.com/features/outbound-calling)
TEAM COLLABORATION
## **Work seamlessly in tandem  
**
Deliver a unified customer experience while working hand in hand with your team.  

  * Loop in relevant team members to support a client
  * Keep your lender partners in sync
  * Avoid scheduling conflicts and overlapping communication


[Learn more about team collaboration](https://www.followupboss.com/features/collaboration-tools)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64677ffe543d669d8bc8a8e6_yellow-icon.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a5cbf8611532c10c1a06d9_collaborators.webp)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a5d304aee9d5727a3578aa_cal2.webp)![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64677ffeda2f60336b8d0a61_blue-icon%20\(2\).svg)
calendar
## **Keep your week on track  
**
Manage all your client appointments and tasks from your computer or phone.  

  * Stay in sync with your Google or Microsoft calendar
  * Send automatic appointment invites and reminders
  * Track appointment types and outcomes
  * Set reminder tasks so you never miss a beat


[Learn more about CALENDAR](https://www.followupboss.com/features/calendar)
integrations
## **Connect all your apps back to an open system  
**
Choose the right apps that work best for your business, and give them a central home inside FUB.  

  * Stay focused by keeping data and processes in FUB
  * Stop jumping between apps to get things done
  * Swap out different tools and stay on top of trends


[see integrations](https://www.followupboss.com/integrations)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d444b114b31ffa0e41bec_Vector%20\(3\).svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a658a524111bab22d3615e_integration-graphic.png)
![man smiling for the camera](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a5f46ac26c7bc79afaae08_tristan-ahumada.webp)
_"The thing we love about Follow Up Boss is the API integration with so many other systems. That's what makes Follow Up Boss so unique."_
Tristan Ahumada
Founder, [Lab Coat Agents](https://www.labcoatagents.com/)
# See a complete view of your business
No matter the size of your team or the complexity of your tech stack, FUB gives you a clear view of how things are going, whether you’re at your desk or on the road.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d445e1c7ee0b5f9e40866_Rectangle%20988.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/66720914d3b6b07f00a6be4c_mobile%201.png)
MOBILE APPS
## **Control everything on the go  
**
Put your entire team’s operating system in your pocket with FUB mobile apps for iOS and Android.  

  * Access detailed lead data and communication from anywhere
  * Stay organized and productive on the road
  * Get notified the second a new lead comes in
  * Make and receive calls, texts and emails


[Learn more about mobile apps](https://www.followupboss.com/features/mobile-apps)
REPORTING
## **Get a clear picture of team success  
**
Always know where your team stands with a complete view of the stats that matter.  

  * Stay focused with a central place to analyze performance.
  * Track metrics like conversion rate and speed to lead over time.
  * Discover opportunities and identify challenges.


[Learn more about reporting](https://www.followupboss.com/features/reporting)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d4437c6662bba9c3aee38_Vector%20\(2\).svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a5d65a5a4159253df9af61_speed-to-lead-2.png)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64677ffef672d8ce2c1237fc_red-icon.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a5d720c254a35d4261d74e_deals.webp)
DEALS
## **Organize & track your team’s deals  
**
Intuitive drag and drop deal pipeline to help visualize and sort your active listings and buyers.  

  * Quickly see which deals are in play
  * View snapshot of key info like close date and commission
  * Forecast your pipeline and measure conversion rate


[Learn more about deals](https://www.followupboss.com/features/deals)
## Finally get everything in one place.  

Try FUB for free, no credit card needed. Join the OS that real estate teams love,   
and we’ll make sure it’s the easiest move you’ll ever make.

## Easy migration  

Switching CRMs doesn't have to be painful. We'll pair you with a FUB expert who specializes in moving data and getting your systems dialed in, so you can hit your goals faster.

Matt Talbot
Owner, [Redefine](https://www.listingmi.com/)

# Build a culture of accountability  

Equip your team with the intelligence and tools they need to hit their goals  

REPORTING
## **Measure your team’s performance**
Get advanced insights into your agents’ performance with pre-built and customizable reports.  

  * Visualize speed to lead, conversion rates and other metrics to identify coaching opportunities
  * Track appointments set by agents and see their outcomes over time
  * Make better decisions by analyzing team’s deal pipeline and monitoring commissions and closings


[Learn more about reporting](https://www.followupboss.com/features/reporting)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d444b114b31ffa0e41bec_Vector%20\(3\).svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a5d61d4747faf08240f4c2_speed-tolead.png)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d445e1c7ee0b5f9e40866_Rectangle%20988.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a5e421cc0875eaf52372bd_leaderboard-v2.webp)
LEADERBOARD
## **Motivate your agents  
**
Make it easy for your team to know where everyone stands and find areas they can improve.  

  * Automatically count up your team’s activity and share it publicly with a beautiful dashboard
  * Power your monthly sales meetings with key analytics
  * Make it known who’s leading in metrics that really matter: Closed deals, volume and GCI


[Learn more about leADERBOARD](https://www.followupboss.com/features/leaderboard)
CALL COACHING
## **Listen to calls for better conversations**
Automatically record your team’s calls, so you can review what went well or what didn’t.  

  * Save a full history of every conversation forever
  * Download and share call recordings for coaching sessions
  * Never forget important details or talking points with AI-powered call summaries & transcripts


[Learn more about calling](https://www.followupboss.com/features/calling)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d4437c6662bba9c3aee38_Vector%20\(2\).svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a5e53487805aee647568d4_call-recording.webp)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64677ffef672d8ce2c1237fc_red-icon.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a5e5e1f93044a42106c56b_mentions.webp)
MENTIONS
## **Spotlight important notes & feedback  
**
Comment directly on your team’s progress to motivate and train them on best practices.  

  * Celebrate victories and give your team kudos
  * Call out areas to improve in real time
  * Collaboratively coach on conversion strategies


[Learn more about team collaboration](https://www.followupboss.com/features/collaboration-tools)

# Superpowers for ISAs
Dial faster, set more appointments and work seamlessly with your agents.
[Start free trial](https://app.followupboss.com/signup/v2)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663de8cff5e7e00a1ff69e_faq-hero-left-img.svg)![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663de8b9f0a97a1b997db9_faq-hero-right-img.svg)

![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a64508896be48024f91ae9_lead-distribution-no-shadow.webp)
lead management**  
**
## Send every lead to the right person
Sync leads from every lead source, then customize your distribution rules to maximize productivity.   

  * Collect leads from 200+ sources
  * Automate lead distribution with custom routing rules
  * Activate drip campaigns automatically


[Learn more about lead routing](https://www.followupboss.com/features/lead-routing)
CALLING AND TEXTING**  
**
## **Make more dials**
Make all your calls and send all your texts right inside your CRM. No need for 3rd party dialers or switching systems to do your follow up.  

  * Live transfer hot leads to an agent’s cell phone
  * Quickly find your best leads
  * Increase response rates with texts after calls


[Learn more about calling](https://www.followupboss.com/features/outbound-calling)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d444b114b31ffa0e41bec_Vector%20\(3\).svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a640bc4faf9d75530aaa14_Calling%20List.webp)
![man smiling for the camera](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646fb16d3ad2f2ac41af065f_5cc760b36b3905a9080c939a_Preston-p-500.jpeg)
_“Our ISA team uses the Follow Up Boss Dialer to make 200-300 calls per day. Calls, appointments, etc… all managed within Follow Up Boss”_
Preston Guyton
Founder, [EZHomeSearch.com](https://ezhomesearch.com/)  

[READ more customer results](https://www.followupboss.com/customer-stories)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d445e1c7ee0b5f9e40866_Rectangle%20988.svg)

CAPTURE INBOUND CALLS**  
**
## Never miss another inbound call
Inbound calls to your business are the hottest leads you can get. Make sure every single one has the best chance of becoming a client.  

  * Route all inbound calls straight into CRM
  * Missed calls and voicemails logged
  * Live transfer hot leads to available agent  



[Learn more about COMPANY NUMBER](https://www.followupboss.com/features/company-number)
EMAIL INTEGRATION
## Sync your email automatically
We automatically sync every single email you exchange with your leads and clients, even if you send from the Mail App on your phone or Outlook on your computer.  

  * No need to remember to use a bcc email
  * Emails sent from your normal email
  * Templates make emailing lightning fast


[Learn more about emailing](https://www.followupboss.com/features/email)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d444b114b31ffa0e41bec_Vector%20\(3\).svg)
![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646fb2d5ad9d162a43200bae_5cc86498c9dcceb2ef7d2db1_Frame4%20\(1\)%20\(1\).webp)

![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a6381615899abfa320a5dd_calendar.webp)
CALENDAR INTEGRATION
## Schedule directly on agents' calendars
Connect your Google and Office 365 integration and see when the agents on your team are available, so you can set appointments without going back and forth.   

  * See availabilities before scheduling
  * Events added to agent's Google or Microsoft calendar
  * Set reminders so nothing gets lost


[Learn more about calendar](https://www.followupboss.com/features/calendar)
COLLABORATION TOOLS
## Keep everyone on track
With features like mentions, collaborators, automated lead assignment and more, it’s a whole lot easier to keep your team organized and on the same page.  

  * Internal conversations on a contact's profile
  * Add lenders to contacts to stay in sync
  * Multiple agents can work on a single contact


[Learn more about collaboration tools](https://www.followupboss.com/features/collaboration-tools)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64677ffe543d669d8bc8a8e6_yellow-icon.svg)

REPORTING
## Results-Focused Reporting
Track everything from response rates to conversion rates with our customizable reporting  

  * Broken down by agents or lead source
  * Speed, response rate and appointments set
  * See which agents are converting deals


[Learn more about reporting](https://www.followupboss.com/features/reporting)

Follow Up Boss is the OS for top-performing solo agents

## Get the same edge as your bigger competitors
The technology that powers leading teams also delivers big results for solo agents who are serious about building a high-performance real estate business.  

  * Test different lead sources & marketing systems
  * Win the speed to lead race
  * Optimize your personal productivity
  * Track & measure everything


[Start free trial](https://app.followupboss.com/signup/v2)

## Be team-ready
Many solo agents who start on Follow Up Boss go on to start teams. If you’re even considering starting a team in the future, we invite you to build your foundation now, on the OS that will grow with you seamlessly.  

  * Upgrade your lead sources, website, and marketing without interrupting your business
  * Add team members later in just a few clicks
  * Learn from our community of succesful team leaders  



[Start free trial](https://app.followupboss.com/signup/v2)
## Win back your day
A serious real estate business isn’t always about maximum growth. If your goals look more like steady commissions with more time to live your life, you’re in the right place.  

  * Respond to leads automatically, even when you're out of the office
  * Easily find and focus on your best opportunities 
  * Fly through your daily work


[Start free trial](https://app.followupboss.com/signup/v2)

## How FUB serves solo agents  

![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/649cfd50ba75cbd927d1ed8f_Integrations%20graphic%20with%20bg.webp)
### Get organized  

Connect all your lead sources, upload your database, and integrate your website. All your leads and clients live in one place, and you get a complete picture of their history on a single timeline.  

![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a642ad5ed3d444b466aa4c_smartlist.webp)
### Stay on top of every lead  

From autoresponders for new leads to personal check-ins with your most important past clients, Action Plans and Smart Lists make sure the right people get the right message at the right time.  

![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a6381615899abfa320a5dd_calendar.webp)
### Work faster  

Make calls, send texts and email your clients in seconds. Integrate your calendar so you can schedule appointments and manage your day. Set up Automations for repetitive tasks. FUB makes any agent’s daily work faster.  

![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a6432a8d980f1140384b77_agent-report-no-shadow.webp)
### Track your progress  

Use the Deals report to manage your pipeline and manage your commissions. Get detailed insights into your marketing campaigns lead source performance so you know what’s working and what’s not.  

![a man smiling for the camera](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d3a7d4b9d3a54b9be9334_5f2857feb50ccb8624ebd51e_sadasdas21213%20\(1\).jpg)
_"Using calling lists, I can make 40 phone calls in 15 minutes. It's a thing of beauty."_
Ryan Fitzgerald
Realtor/Owner, [Raleigh Realty](https://www.raleighrealtyhomes.com/)  

[READ more customer results](https://www.followupboss.com/customer-stories)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d445e1c7ee0b5f9e40866_Rectangle%20988.svg)

Connect your other systems to build the technology stack that fits your business and your goals. Follow Up Boss is the hub of it all.  

  * 250+ integrations (and counting)
  * Connect your favorite IDX website platform
  * Open API & constant development  

  * Embedded apps

# Work in your business, not on it
The Real Estate Team OS turns chaos to calm and puts you in the driver's seat.
[Start free trial](https://app.followupboss.com/signup/v2)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663de8cff5e7e00a1ff69e_faq-hero-left-img.svg)![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663de8b9f0a97a1b997db9_faq-hero-right-img.svg)
LEAD MANAGEMENT**  
**
## Get organized
Advanced lead management helps you win the speed to lead race and stop letting leads fall through the cracks.  

  * Import leads from 200+ sources in real time
  * Set up routing rules by lead source or type
  * Respond immediately with Action Plans
  * Track marketing campaigns


[Learn more about Lead Flow](https://www.followupboss.com/features/lead-routing)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d444b114b31ffa0e41bec_Vector%20\(3\).svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a63023b22dc2d1b5bd93ab_smartlist-and-activity.webp)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d445e1c7ee0b5f9e40866_Rectangle%20988.svg)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a6315a0a0445c0a83a404d_calling-smartlist.webp)
AGENT PRODUCTIVIty**  
**
## Make every agent more productive
Agents love FUB because it makes them better at their jobs, and they see it in their commission checks.   

  * Zero in on the best opportunities
  * Automate follow up & busywork
  * Keep every communication in one place
  * Collaborate with teammates  



[See why agents love Follow Up Boss](https://www.followupboss.com/how-it-works/agent)
TRACKING & REPORTING
## Build motivation and accountability
Use automatic tracking and built-in reporting to know who’s performing, find coaching opportunities and motivate your team.  

  * Track activity automatically, log notes easily
  * Measure conversion rates by agent & source
  * Follow leads all the way to closed deals
  * Record calls for coaching  



[Learn more about reporting](https://www.followupboss.com/features/reporting)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646d4437c6662bba9c3aee38_Vector%20\(2\).svg)

OPEN PLATFORM**  
**
## Evolve with ease
Connect your other systems to build the technology stack that fits your business, and make changes whenever you need to.  

  * 250+ integrations (and counting)
  * Connect your IDX website
  * Open API & constant development  

  * Embedded apps  



[What's an "Open System?"](https://www.followupboss.com/open)

Kris Lindahl

TEAM OS FEATURES
## Build the systems you need to scale  

![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/649cfd50ba75cbd927d1ed8f_Integrations%20graphic%20with%20bg.webp)
### 250+ integrations  

From lead sources to marketing platforms to transaction management, Follow Up Boss [connects](https://www.followupboss.com/integrations) to all your tools and tech.  

![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a633f915899abfa31c9023_Lead-distribution.webp)
### Lead routing  

Sync with lead sources in real-time and [distribute leads](https://www.followupboss.com/features/lead-routing) instantly. Set up unique rules for different sources and lead types.  

![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a637a936e8f756c95bd04a_action-plan-short.webp)
### Action plans  

[Automate follow up](https://www.followupboss.com/features/action-plans) to your new leads and database with drip emails, task reminders and triggers to third party integrations.  

![graphical user interface, text, application
](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e02f7c6789932add46dc6_60245b7abe9e0d15cced8f14_Mobile%20calling%20screenshot.webp)
### Complete phone system with texting  

Track inbound calls and route them to the right agents, dial through lead lists in a flash and text (including group text) [from one place](https://www.followupboss.com/features/calling).  

![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a6381615899abfa320a5dd_calendar.webp)
### Email & calendar sync  

Sync with Google and Office 365 to send, receive and track emails and manage your calendar and appointments right from your CRM.  

![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a6397db22dc2d1b5c7da83_Activity%20extra%20short.webp)
### Website tracking  

[Track lead activity](https://www.followupboss.com/features/pixel) on any IDX website and customize your follow up based on what properties and pages they viewed.  

![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a63b2a0a0445c0a8456e98_reporting-simple.webp)
### Reporting  

Measure the things that matter. [Report](https://www.followupboss.com/features/reporting) on lead source performance and agent metrics or motivate your team with [Leaderboard](https://www.followupboss.com/features/leaderboard).  

![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64a63ec7f740ca49698c23f7_team-collaboration-2.webp)
### Collaboration  

Work with teammates to get deals closed. See a lead’s full activity history, [share inboxes](https://www.followupboss.com/features/team-inbox) and @ mention people to get their attention.  

![man smiling for the camera](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646fad55080e747f5bf8eb36_5f5bf7ab13e49b75d8effdfd_Robert%20Slack%20Testimonial%20Headshot%20\(1\)-p-1080.jpeg)
_“We’ve had Follow Up Boss since day one. We’re in all of Florida so we can’t do a round robin. We need to distribute by zip code and Follow Up Boss makes that easy.”_
Robert Slack
Founder & CEO, [Robert Slack Fine Homes](https://robertslack.com/)  
#1 Realtor.com lead buyer in America

  * Capture notes in the field

ISA SUPPORT
## Get more from ISAs
Make it easy for everyone in your office to get more done.  

  * Create dedicated call lists for different agents and teams.
  * Filter on lead type, lead source, or any other data you have.
  * ISAs can easily transfer calls to an agent’s cell phone for seamless handoffs
  * Customize reports for each team or group  



[How ISAs use Follow Up Boss](https://www.followupboss.com/how-it-works/isa)

BUILT FOR TEAMS
## Teams move fast
Lead sources come and go. Marketing tactics evolve. Brokerage affiliations aren’t forever. When you're building a team, things change. We make change easy.  

  * Import leads from any source, connect any website
  * Change parts of your system without interrupting your team
  * Build systems to automate your team's unique process


[How FUB Serves teams](https://www.followupboss.com/how-it-works/team-leader)
MORE CHOICE
## All-in-ones hold you back
Most real estate CRMs are part of all-in-one solutions that lock you into using what they're selling. The Real Estate Team OS is a flexible hub that lets you choose your tools and make them work together. [](https://www.followupboss.com/integrations)  

  * Convert more leads with world-class CRM features
  * Choose from 250+ lead source and integrations for everything else
  * Stay flexible & move fast to capture new opportunities


[Explore integrations](https://www.followupboss.com/integrations)

CONSTANT INNOVATION
## FUB pushes you forward
Our engineering team is 100% focused on building a world-class real estate CRM, with an open API and a thriving ecosystem of integrations: An operating system for real estate businesses.  

  * New CRM features weekly
  * Integrations added regularly
  * You're never locked in to things you don't want to use


[LATEST UPDATES](https://updates.followupboss.com)
ENDLESS CONNECTIONS
## What makes Follow Up Boss "open?"  

![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/649cfd50ba75cbd927d1ed8f_Integrations%20graphic%20with%20bg.webp)
### 250+ integrations  

Integrate your CRM with 200+ real estate [lead sources](https://www.followupboss.com/integrations?tag=Lead+Providers) and over 30 other [software vendors](https://www.followupboss.com/integrations) so your leads are in one place and your systems work in sync.  

![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646e3f2b097cd68ffc9cab3d_5efa51011667fccfa77ad678_Frame%2033.webp)
### Website pixel  

Add the [Follow Up Boss Pixel](https://www.followupboss.com/features/pixel) to your IDX website and see the properties a lead viewed and pages they visited. Track Facebook and Google ads automatically and see which leads actually closed.  

![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646fac02814367f7b002f9f2_5fdbe78721586a56fc40a4d8_popular%20zaps%20\(1\).png)
### Zapier connections  

Automate your business with a deep [Zapier integration](https://www.followupboss.com/integrations/zapier) that connects your Follow Up Boss account to thousands of other software vendors in seconds.  

![graphical user interface, text, application](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646fd3cf99dce5d6ebfe9384_5fda98fbfa31d8cdba7399d4_follow-up-boss-embedded-app-cropped%202%20\(1\)-p-1080.webp)
### Open API & embedded apps  

APIs let software systems talk to each other. Our API is open, so any vendor can create an integration. We even have embedded apps so they can build apps you use right inside of Follow Up Boss.  

![man smiling for the camera](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646fac2aad9d162a4319adce_5fdce8f65784ee0d620f4cef_kyle%20whissel%20website%20testimonial%20\(1\).webp)
_"We love Follow Up Boss because they play nice with virtually everybody out there...they'll integrate with everybody. Follow Up Boss is the hub of everything for us and then we add spokes from there."_
Kyle Whissel
Team Leader, [Whissel Realty Group](https://whisselrealty.com/)  

CUSTOMER-FOCUSED
## Open isn't just tech. It's everything.  

customer focused 
## We answer to you
Follow Up Boss moves fast, stays focused on the long-term and does what’s right for customers. Get unlimited access to our US-based product expert team on all plans. We're real humans who pick up your call (or return your email).

HONEST
## Transparent pricing, no contracts
You have to earn your client’s business on every transaction. Why should we be any different? Follow Up Boss has clear pricing, no hidden fees and no contracts. You can upgrade, downgrade, or cancel any time (and take your data with you), right inside the app.  

[SEE PRICING](https://www.followupboss.com/pricing)

## It’s different at FUB
![colored image in frame](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646fa87d7166f8602d01707f_Layer_1.svg)
### We only answer to you
That gives us the freedom to focus on a long-term vision for serving the real estate community.
![colored stacking card](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646fa87ca59d8eefe63ccd0a_Platform.svg)
### Open platform
Growing businesses need flexibility. Follow Up Boss is dedicated to being the best real estate CRM and making it easy for you to plug in the website, lead gen and marketing systems that make sense for your needs today, and then swap them out when things change.
![colored card airplain](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/646fa87c47a4284982937ae6_Innovation.svg)
### Constant innovation
How do we stay ahead of the competition? Easy: By keeping you a step ahead of your competition. That's why we release new CRM features and updates every single week.

# THE CONVERSION SUMMIT  

#  Listings Workshop  

Join us for a free, one-day virtual summit that will teach you how to **get more listings** and **rise above the challenges of the market**. This year’s Conversion Summit is laser-focused on sellers, giving you the tools and strategies to gain a competitive advantage in 2024.  

**Wednesday, December 13th 2023  
** from 10:00am - 2:00pm PST / 1:00pm - 5:00pm EST  

![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663de8cff5e7e00a1ff69e_faq-hero-left-img.svg)![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/64663de8b9f0a97a1b997db9_faq-hero-right-img.svg)
**What you’ll learn  
**
## **When more sellers are staying put, how do you win listings?**
Think your success is at the mercy of the housing market? Think again. High interest rates and low inventory come with unique challenges – Conversion Summit will empower you to meet them head-on.  

Walk away with a playbook for:  

  * Generating listings from your existing database
  * Creating a powerful listing presentation that wins
  * Building the key relationships you need to develop to generate listings
  * What’s working for top producers right now (and what everyone is doing wrong)


[Schedule a Demo](https://www.followupboss.com/features/email)
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/6553f18ddf2cafe5a88e9f68_Group%201171276820%20\(1\).webp)
**CHAOS TO CALM  
**
## Your biggest questions, answered.
We asked the FUB community and combed social media to find your most burning questions and uncertainties about sellers, listings and the market. Every session is designed specifically to solve the common challenges that agents face today.  

![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/655550c3a8b87ce0b6c83688_Group%201171276828%20\(1\).webp)
Speakers
## Hear from the best in the business.
Each session will feature top producers, team owners and leading coaches who know how to create opportunity in every market. Get different perspectives and choose the approaches that are a fit for your business and 2024 goals.

FAQ
Is this really a free-event?
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/65562aaf837f48a1862ffd68_Vector%20\(3\).svg)
Yes! It’s really free. You asked, and we delivered. However, space is limited, so we kindly request that you register only if you plan to attend.
Will the sessions be recorded?
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/65562aaf837f48a1862ffd68_Vector%20\(3\).svg)
No, the sessions will not be recorded. This is a free, in-person event that will bring tons of great content. Space is limited, so register now to reserve your spot!
Is this a hands-on FUB training session?
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/65562aaf837f48a1862ffd68_Vector%20\(3\).svg)
No. Although our speakers will cover various topics (all related to conversion) we don’t have any hands-on training during this event. If you’re hungry for more FUB training, check out our upcoming webinars.
I have a question that's not answered here.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/65562aaf837f48a1862ffd68_Vector%20\(3\).svg)
You have questions, we've got answers! Send an email to conversionsummit@zillowgroup.com and we'd be happy to help.

# Your contacts are on Zillow. Meet them there.
Stop flying blind and start showing up right when they need you with Zillow Pro—the premium membership for Follow Up Boss agents.
[Contact sales](https://www.zillow.com/agents/contact/)
## A Zillow Pro membership adds Zillow's exclusive buyer intent data, AI-powered follow-up, and premium branding to Follow Up Boss.
Any agent can create a free Zillow Pro account to access basic tools—upgrading to a Zillow Pro membership unlocks the full suite of premium capabilities.

## How it works
With a Zillow Pro membership, it all starts with a My Agent relationship.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/697d1149db9a2ae6e30c2860_connect-icon.svg)
Connect with your contacts
Any lead, from any source. Just send a My Agent invitation through Follow Up Boss with a few clicks.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/697d11495aa8f3d86523e537_see-icon.svg)
See their Zillow activity,  
brand their experience
Once they accept your connection request, you’re their exclusive local agent on Zillow for your market. They’ll see your brand on Zillow and you get access to their Zillow activity.
![](https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/644fec6f9633da07ea1323cb/697d11493bd41fd27a9177c9_reach-icon.svg)
Reach out with perfect context
The contact’s Zillow activity gets sent back to Follow Up Boss, where it powers Smart Lists, AI-generated messaging and more. Stop flying blind and start following up smarter.
My Agent relationships are exclusive for each market. Once a contact accepts your request, you’re the agent they see on Zillow in your local market unless they decide to break the relationship.
